# Function ledger

Generated by `scripts/ledger` (docs/ENGINE_PORT_PLAN.md, Phase 0); do not edit.
Regenerate with:

```sh
cargo run --release --manifest-path scripts/ledger/Cargo.toml
```

Every function of FalloutNV.exe 1.4.0.525 from the engine map
(`research/engine-map/engine_map.tsv`; how it is made: `research/engine-map/README.md`)
gets one status. Names, units and subsystems come from the Xbox 360 prototype
(Xbox PDB, ADR-0002), matched to PC functions as described there.

Map provenance:

- FalloutNV.exe 1.4.0.525 (unpacked), SHA-256 19406942e48724d797300c4ea6be9ac69a32f670b8c35db09279a2258422739f
- Names and units: Xbox PDB Fallout_Release_MemDebug.pdb (GUID 036BA387-1386-4F96-A709-DA70225CDF94, age 1), ADR-0002
- Generated by research/engine-map (match, map) from NvEngineMap.java output; see its README

| Status | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| translated | cited on a translation marker (ADR-0003) in an engine crate (`crates/engine*`) |
| static init (table) | a static initializer or an `atexit` destructor run from a generated table (`crates/engine-core/data/static_initializers.tsv`, `crates/engine-core/data/atexit_destructors.tsv`; research/initgen), not a hand translation |
| traced | cited elsewhere in Rust, including a translation marker outside the engine crates (the viewer describes it, the engine still needs it) |
| platform | the platform layer stands in for it: OS glue, the Direct3D 9 / D3DX libraries, Xbox system services (the renderer is game code, ADR-0007) |
| library | compiler-generated code, C/C++ runtime, zlib |
| open | none of the above |

### Frame system wiring

Every depth-1 and depth-2 call is listed in [frame_wiring.tsv](../research/engine-map/frame_wiring.tsv), generated and checked by `world::frame::coverage`. Partial means some behavior exists, with its placement or missing work stated in the row; it does not mean the function is fully executed in play. Queries and unsupported calls stay open. Translation counts above are separate.

| Depth | system | partial | open |
| ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 1 | 1 | 12 | 130 |
| 2 | 0 | 19 | 1556 |
